# Pi release adoption audit

## Scope and evidence

- Reviewed: the complete packaged changelog from 0.10.0 through 0.85.1 (274 releases, 2804 bullets).
- Target and active CLI: 0.85.1; registry latest at collection: 0.85.1.
- Repository checkpoint: `ad3e87c19010e5b33de873a148f5f590acfbf570`.
- Collection time: 2026-09-13; complete collector evidence: [`evidence/pi-release-0.10.0-through-0.85.1.json`](evidence/pi-release-0.10.0-through-0.85.1.json).
- Evidence SHA-256: `59a5d6c668b81911d449fb9f22b0ca4525f58f0544a620bb5811239e4120fd3d`.
- Changelog source: `@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent@0.85.1/CHANGELOG.md`; target-linked documentation and hashes are embedded in the JSON evidence.
- Starting tree already had unrelated changes in `README.md`, `docs/README.md`, `docs/reference/ZWIFT_DOMAIN.md`, and `docs/tutorial/getting-started.md`, plus untracked `STATUS.md`, `TASKS.md`, and `docs/howto/RETURN_TO_RACING.md`. They were preserved.

## Repository assessment

The project is a Rust application and does not declare or install Pi packages. The collector found no manifest-declared Pi resources. Direct inspection found no project `.pi` or `.agents` resources, SDK/RPC integration, Pi launcher, extension, skill, prompt template, theme, or Pi settings. Generic matches such as Raspberry Pi, Rust constants named `PI`, JavaScript files, “skill,” and ordinary CLI flags are not Pi integrations.

The only live Pi-facing repository surface is `AGENTS.md`, which target 0.85.1 continues to load as a context file. The former `CLAUDE.md` contained only a pointer to `AGENTS.md`; it was removed under the active machine policy that `CLAUDE.md` is deprecated, not because Pi 0.85.1 removed support. The duplicate generated aggregate `docs/ALL_DOCUMENTATION.md` was later removed during documentation rationalisation because its source pages remain canonical. The tracked `.claude/settings.local.json` was inspected but is vendor-specific permission history, not a Pi resource, and was not changed.

## Result

| Disposition | Count |
|---|---:|
| change required | 0 |
| already covered | 0 |
| checked—no change | 25 |
| no local surface | 2779 |
| follow-up required | 0 |

No changelog bullet requires a repository code, configuration, package, or minimum-version change. No follow-up is required for Pi compatibility.
